  // Micro-interaction for passenger chip switching
  const pax1Chip = document.getElementById('pax-1-chip');
  const pax2Chip = document.getElementById('pax-2-chip');

  if (pax1Chip && pax2Chip) {
    pax1Chip.addEventListener('click', () => {
      pax1Chip.classList.add('bg-primary-fixed/20', 'shadow-md');
      pax2Chip.classList.remove('bg-primary-fixed/20', 'shadow-md');
    });

    pax2Chip.addEventListener('click', () => {
      pax2Chip.classList.add('bg-primary-fixed/20', 'shadow-md');
      pax1Chip.classList.remove('bg-primary-fixed/20', 'shadow-md');
    });
  }
</script></main></body></html>

<script>
  function handleConfirm() {
    const consent = document.getElementById('exitConsent');
    if (consent && !consent.checked) {
      alert('لطفاً شرایط مربوط به ردیف خروج اضطراری را تأیید نمایید.');
      consent.focus();
      return;
    }
    const btn = document.getElementById('confirmBtn');
    btn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px] animate-spin">progress_activity</span><span>در حال اتصال به درگاه...</span>';
    btn.disabled = true;
    setTimeout(() => {
      alert('پیش‌فاکتور تأیید شد. به درگاه پرداخت نمایشی متصل شدید.');
      btn.innerHTML = '<span>تأیید شد ✓</span>';
    }, 1000);
  }
</script></main></body></html>

<script>
  function handleConfirm() {
    const consent = document.getElementById('exitConsent');
    if (consent && !consent.checked) {
      alert('لطفاً شرایط مربوط به ردیف خروج اضطراری را تأیید نمایید.');
      consent.focus();
      return;
    }
    const btn = document.getElementById('confirmBtn');
    btn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px] animate-spin">progress_activity</span><span>در حال اتصال به درگاه...</span>';
    btn.disabled = true;
    setTimeout(() => {
      alert('پیش‌فاکتور تأیید شد. به درگاه پرداخت نمایشی متصل شدید.');
      btn.innerHTML = '<span>تأیید شد ✓</span>';
    }, 1000);
  }
</script></main></body></html>

<script>
  function handleConfirm() {
    const consent = document.getElementById('exitConsent');
    if (consent && !consent.checked) {
      alert('لطفاً شرایط مربوط به ردیف خروج اضطراری را تأیید نمایید.');
      consent.focus();
      return;
    }
    const btn = document.getElementById('confirmBtn');
    btn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px] animate-spin">progress_activity</span><span>در حال اتصال به درگاه...</span>';
    btn.disabled = true;
    setTimeout(() => {
      alert('پیش‌فاکتور تأیید شد. به درگاه پرداخت نمایشی متصل شدید.');
      btn.innerHTML = '<span>تأیید شد ✓</span>';
    }, 1000);
  }
</script></main></body></html>

<script>
  // Simple Native Micro-interactions
  (function initFilters() {
    // Range Slider formatting
    const priceSlider = document.getElementById('price-slider');
    const priceDisplay = document.getElementById('price-display');

    if (priceSlider && priceDisplay) {
      priceSlider.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
        const val = Number(e.target.value);
        const formatted = new Intl.NumberFormat('fa-IR').format(val);
        priceDisplay.innerHTML = `${formatted} <span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm font-normal text-on-surface-variant">تومان</span>`;
      });
    }

    // Toggle Buttons
    document.querySelectorAll('.filter-toggle').forEach(btn => {
      btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const isActive = btn.getAttribute('data-active') === 'true';
        const thumb = btn.querySelector('span');
        if (isActive) {
          btn.setAttribute('data-active', 'false');
          btn.classList.remove('bg-primary');
          btn.classList.add('bg-surface-container-highest');
          thumb.classList.remove('translate-x-[-20px]');
          thumb.classList.add('translate-x-0');
        } else {
          btn.setAttribute('data-active', 'true');
          btn.classList.add('bg-primary');
          btn.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-highest');
          thumb.classList.add('translate-x-[-20px]');
          thumb.classList.remove('translate-x-0');
        }
      });
    });

    // Stops Segmented Chips Single Select
    const stopChips = document.querySelectorAll('.stop-chip');
    stopChips.forEach(chip => {
      chip.addEventListener('click', () => {
        stopChips.forEach(c => {
          c.classList.remove('bg-secondary', 'text-on-secondary');
          c.classList.add('bg-surface-container', 'text-on-surface');
        });
        chip.classList.add('bg-secondary', 'text-on-secondary');
        chip.classList.remove('bg-surface-container', 'text-on-surface');
      });
    });

    // Time Chips
    const timeChips = document.querySelectorAll('.time-chip');
    timeChips.forEach(chip => {
      chip.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const isSelected = chip.classList.contains('bg-secondary');
        const iconDiv = chip.querySelector('div > div');
        const check = chip.querySelector('.checkmark');
        
        if (isSelected) {
          chip.classList.remove('bg-secondary', 'text-on-secondary');
          chip.classList.add('bg-surface-container', 'text-on-surface');
          if (check) check.classList.add('opacity-0');
        } else {
          chip.classList.add('bg-secondary', 'text-on-secondary');
          chip.classList.remove('bg-surface-container', 'text-on-surface');
          if (check) check.classList.remove('opacity-0');
        }
      });
    });

    // Baggage Chips Single Select
    const baggageChips = document.querySelectorAll('.baggage-chip');
    baggageChips.forEach(chip => {
      chip.addEventListener('click', () => {
        baggageChips.forEach(b => {
          b.classList.remove('bg-secondary', 'text-on-secondary', 'font-bold');
          b.classList.add('bg-surface-container', 'text-on-surface');
        });
        chip.classList.add('bg-secondary', 'text-on-secondary', 'font-bold');
        chip.classList.remove('bg-surface-container', 'text-on-surface');
      });
    });

    // Dismiss Sheet Demo
    const sheet = document.getElementById('filter-sheet');
    const closeBtn = document.getElementById('close-sheet-btn');
    const applyBtn = document.getElementById('apply-filters-btn');

    const closeHandler = () => {
      if (sheet) {
        sheet.classList.add('translate-y-full');
        setTimeout(() => {
          sheet.classList.remove('translate-y-full');
        }, 800);
      }
    };

    if (closeBtn) closeBtn.addEventListener('click', closeHandler);
    if (applyBtn) applyBtn.addEventListener('click', closeHandler);

    // Reset All Demo
    const resetBtn = document.getElementById('reset-all-btn');
    const footerClear = document.getElementById('footer-clear-btn');
    const resetAction = () => {
      if (priceSlider) {
        priceSlider.value = '25000000';
        priceSlider.dispatchEvent(new Event('input'));
      }
      document.querySelectorAll('input[type="checkbox"]').forEach(c => c.checked = false);
    };
    if (resetBtn) resetBtn.addEventListener('click', resetAction);
    if (footerClear) footerClear.addEventListener('click', resetAction);
  })();
</script></main></body></html>

<script>
  (function initBottomSheetInteractions() {
    const sheet = document.getElementById('bottom-sheet');
    const backdrop = document.getElementById('modal-backdrop');
    const closeBtn = document.getElementById('close-sheet-btn');
    const applyBtn = document.getElementById('apply-filters-btn');
    const clearBtn = document.getElementById('clear-all-filters');
    const resetBtn = document.getElementById('reset-filters-btn');

    // Close Modal Handler
    function dismissModal() {
      sheet.classList.add('translate-y-full');
      backdrop.classList.add('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
      setTimeout(() => {
        sheet.style.display = 'none';
        backdrop.style.display = 'none';
        const blurredContent = document.querySelector('.filter.blur-sm');
        if (blurredContent) {
          blurredContent.classList.remove('blur-sm', 'brightness-75');
        }
      }, 300);
    }

    if (closeBtn) closeBtn.addEventListener('click', dismissModal);
    if (backdrop) backdrop.addEventListener('click', dismissModal);
    if (applyBtn) {
      applyBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        applyBtn.innerHTML = 'در حال جستجو...';
        setTimeout(dismissModal, 400);
      });
    }

    // Toggle multi-select chips
    const filterChips = document.querySelectorAll('.filter-chip');
    filterChips.forEach(chip => {
      chip.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const isSelected = chip.getAttribute('data-selected') === 'true';
        if (isSelected) {
          chip.setAttribute('data-selected', 'false');
          chip.classList.remove('bg-secondary', 'text-on-secondary');
          chip.classList.add('bg-surface-container', 'text-on-surface');
        } else {
          chip.setAttribute('data-selected', 'true');
          chip.classList.add('bg-secondary', 'text-on-secondary');
          chip.classList.remove('bg-surface-container', 'text-on-surface');
        }
      });
    });

    // Reset / Clear logic
    function resetAll() {
      filterChips.forEach(chip => {
        chip.setAttribute('data-selected', 'false');
        chip.classList.remove('bg-secondary', 'text-on-secondary');
        chip.classList.add('bg-surface-container', 'text-on-surface');
      });
      const checkboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.toggle-checkbox');
      checkboxes.forEach(cb => {
        cb.checked = false;
        const track = cb.parentElement.querySelector('.toggle-track');
        const thumb = cb.parentElement.querySelector('.toggle-thumb');
        if (track && thumb) {
          track.classList.replace('bg-primary', 'bg-surface-container-highest');
          thumb.classList.remove('-translate-x-6');
          thumb.classList.replace('bg-on-primary', 'bg-on-surface-variant');
        }
      });
      const badge = document.querySelector('.bg-secondary-container');
      if (badge) badge.textContent = '۰ فیلتر فعال';
      if (applyBtn) applyBtn.querySelector('span').textContent = 'اعمال فیلترها (۴۲ اقامتگاه)';
    }

    if (clearBtn) clearBtn.addEventListener('click', resetAll);
    if (resetBtn) resetBtn.addEventListener('click', resetAll);

    // Dynamic Switch Toggle interactions
    const toggles = document.querySelectorAll('.toggle-checkbox');
    toggles.forEach(cb => {
      cb.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
        const parent = cb.parentElement;
        const track = parent.querySelector('.toggle-track');
        const thumb = parent.querySelector('.toggle-thumb');
        if (e.target.checked) {
          track.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-highest');
          track.classList.add('bg-primary');
          thumb.classList.add('-translate-x-6');
          thumb.classList.remove('bg-on-surface-variant');
          thumb.classList.add('bg-on-primary');
        } else {
          track.classList.add('bg-surface-container-highest');
          track.classList.remove('bg-primary');
          thumb.classList.remove('-translate-x-6');
          thumb.classList.add('bg-on-surface-variant');
          thumb.classList.remove('bg-on-primary');
        }
      });
    });
  })();
</script></main></body></html>

  // Simple interactive micro-interactions for swap & trip types
  (function initHomeScreenInteractions() {
    const swapBtn = document.getElementById('swap-route-btn');
    const oneWayBtn = document.getElementById('btn-one-way');
    const roundTripBtn = document.getElementById('btn-round-trip');

    if (swapBtn) {
      swapBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        this.classList.toggle('rotate-180');
      });
    }

    if (oneWayBtn && roundTripBtn) {
      oneWayBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        oneWayBtn.className = 'px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-secondary-container text-on-secondary-fixed-variant font-label-sm text-label-sm font-semibold';
        roundTripBtn.className = 'px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-surface-container text-on-surface-variant font-label-sm text-label-sm font-medium';
      });

      roundTripBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        roundTripBtn.className = 'px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-secondary-container text-on-secondary-fixed-variant font-label-sm text-label-sm font-semibold';
        oneWayBtn.className = 'px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-surface-container text-on-surface-variant font-label-sm text-label-sm font-medium';
      });
    }
  })();
